     import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
     import { AppModule } from './app.module';
     import { buildSwaggerDocument } from './swagger/swagger-document';
     
     export async function exportSpec(outputPath = 'openapi.json'): Promise<void> {
       const app = await NestFactory.create(AppModule, { logger: false });
       const document = buildSwaggerDocument(app);
       writeFileSync(outputPath, JSON.stringify(document, null, 2));
       await app.close();
     }
     
     if (require.main === module) {
       void exportSpec();
     }
     swagger-document.ts
     import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
     import { DocumentBuilder, SwaggerModule } from '@nestjs/swagger';
     import { ApiErrorEnvelope } from '../common/openapi/api-error-envelope.dto';
     
     export function buildSwaggerConfig() {
       return new DocumentBuilder()
         .setTitle('StreamTube API')
         .setDescription('API REST do StreamTube')
         .setVersion('1.0')
         .addBearerAuth(
           { type: 'http', scheme: 'bearer', bearerFormat: 'JWT' },
           'access-token',
         )
         .build();
     }
     
     export function buildSwaggerDocument(app: INestApplication) {
       return SwaggerModule.createDocument(app, buildSwaggerConfig(), {
         extraModels: [ApiErrorEnvelope],
       });
     }

  ⎿  5:date: 2026-05-12
     18:## TD-01: OpenAPI Documentation Tooling
     29:- Pacote oficial do NestJS, baseado em decoradores (`@ApiProperty`, `@ApiResponse`, `@ApiTags`) e gerador de OpenAPI a partir de `DocumentBuilder`. Suporta um CLI plugin (`nest-cli.json` → `plugins`) que faz **introspecção automática** de DTOs com `classValidatorShim: true`, reduzindo decoradores explícitos para o mínimo.
     30:- **Pros:** Integração nativa com `class-validator`/`class-transformer` (já no stack). Curva de aprendizado baixa para times com NestJS. CLI plugin elimina ~80% do boilerplate quando configurado. Ecosistema maduro: Swagger UI embarcada, JSON/YAML export prontos, suporte a auth schemes (Bearer/JWT) out-of-the-box. Versão `@nestjs/swagger ^11` é compatível com `@nestjs/core ^11.0.1` instalado.
     31:- **Cons:** Requer alguma anotação adicional para responses complexas, polimorfismo, e variações por status. Spec depende de runtime metadata (decorators) — não há "pure type-driven" sem o CLI plugin.
     43:**Recommendation:** **Option A (`@nestjs/swagger`)** — é a única opção que preserva as decisões anteriores (`class-validator` em TD-06 de phase-02-auth) sem re-platform; o CLI plugin com `classValidatorShim: true` aproveita os decoradores `class-validator` existentes para inferir schemas, mantendo o boilerplate baixo. Nestia tem mérito técnico real mas o custo de migração do stack de validação inviabiliza-a sem uma decisão upstream de supersede de TD-06. Manual authoring é descartado.
     45:**Decision:** A (@nestjs/swagger + CLI plugin)
     48:**Revisions:**
     50:- 2026-05-12 — Esclarece que o CLI plugin (`classValidatorShim: true`) cobre apenas inferência de schemas de DTOs a partir de `class-validator`; documentação de operações, respostas tipadas por status code, contratos de erro (alinhados ao envelope de phase-02-auth/TD-07) e exemplos exigem decoradores explícitos (`@ApiOperation`, `@ApiResponse`, `@ApiBody`, `@ApiParam`, `@ApiQuery`, `@ApiExtraModels`). _Rationale:_ openapi.json gerado pelo bo … (+532 chars)
● Read(/Users/ricardorauberpereira/Downloads/MBA-Desafios/Upload_Processamento_Videos-StreamTube/docs/decisions/technical-decisions-openapi-docs-nestjs.md)
  ⎿  54	## TD-02: OpenAPI Spec Artifact Strategy
     55	
     56	**Scope:** Cross-layer
     57	
     58	**Trigger:** Definir como o spec OpenAPI é exposto para consumidores — humanos (exploração interativa) e máquinas (codegen de cliente FE futuro).
     59	
     60	**Context:** O `@nestjs/swagger` (TD-01, Option A) permite duas modalidades de publicação do spec: **runtime** (Swagger UI + endpoint JSON servidos pela aplicação Nest) e **artefato estático** (arquivo `openapi.json` gerado em build/script e commitado ou publicado em CI). A escolha afeta como o frontend (futuro) consome o contrato: se o spec só existe em runtime, codegen exige a API no ar; se existe como arquivo, codegen roda offline em qualquer CI. Como o `next-frontend` ainda não integra o spec mas vai integrar, a decisão é cross-layer — o artefato é o ponto de contato entre os subprojetos.
     61	
     62	**Options:**
     63	
     64	### Option A: Runtime-only (Swagger UI + endpoint JSON, sem arquivo)
     65	- `SwaggerModule.setup('api/docs', app, document)` monta UI em `/api/docs` e o JSON em `/api/docs-json`. Nenhum arquivo é gerado/commitado.
     66	- **Pros:** Setup mínimo (um bloco em `main.ts`). Spec sempre reflete o código rodando — zero risco de drift entre arquivo e runtime. Sem etapa de build adicional.
     67	- **Cons:** Codegen do FE exige a API rodando no momento (CI precisa subir o backend ou apontar para uma instância estável). Sem histórico do contrato em diff de PR — mudanças de schema são invisíveis até alguém comparar specs em runtime.
     68	
     69	### Option B: Artefato estático apenas (gerar `openapi.json`, sem UI runtime)
     70	- Script `npm run openapi:gen` que instancia o app NestJS em modo "build-only", chama `SwaggerModule.createDocument`, serializa `JSON.stringify(document)` em `openapi.json` (ou em `<backend-subproject>/openapi.json`) e encerra. UI runtime desativada. Arquivo commitado ou publicado em CI.
     71	- **Pros:** Contrato versionado — mudanças aparecem como diff em PR. Codegen do FE roda offline. Auditoria de breaking changes facilitada. … (+1258 chars)

     420	        if (ts.isNumericLiteral(init))
     421	            return Number(init.text) < 400;
     422	        if (ts.isStringLiteral(init)) {
     423	            return (init.text === '1XX' ||
     424	                init.text === '2XX' ||
     425	                init.text === '3XX' ||
     426	                init.text === 'default');
     427	        }
     428	        return true;
     429	    }

  ⎿  1	import { applyDecorators, Header, Redirect } from '@nestjs/common';
     2	import { ApiOperation, ApiResponse, getSchemaPath } from '@nestjs/swagger';
     3	import { ApiErrorEnvelope } from '../../common/openapi/api-error-envelope.dto';
     4	
     5	/**
     6	 * Shared shape of the delivery routes: a 302 to a presigned storage URL that
     7	 * must not be cached (it is a portable credential until it expires).
     8	 */
     9	export function ApiDeliveryRedirect(
     10	  summary: string,
     11	  description: string,
     12	  notReadyDescription = 'VIDEO_NOT_READY — the video is not ready',
     13	): MethodDecorator {
     14	  return applyDecorators(
     15	    // The handler returns the target URL; the decorator only sets the default
     16	    Redirect(),
     17	    Header('Cache-Control', 'private, no-store'),
     18	    ApiOperation({ summary, description }),
     19	    ApiResponse({
     20	      status: 302,
     21	      description: 'Redirect to a short-lived presigned storage URL',
     22	      headers: {
     23	        Location: { schema: { type: 'string', format: 'uri' } },
     24	        'Cache-Control': {
     25	          schema: { type: 'string', example: 'private, no-store' },
     26	        },
     27	      },
     28	    }),
     29	    ApiResponse({
     30	      status: 400,
     31	      description: 'VALIDATION_ERROR — `id` is not a UUID',
     32	      schema: { $ref: getSchemaPath(ApiErrorEnvelope) },
     33	    }),
     34	    ApiResponse({
     35	      status: 401,
     36	      description: 'Missing or invalid access token',
     37	      schema: { $ref: getSchemaPath(ApiErrorEnvelope) },
     38	    }),
     39	    ApiResponse({
     40	      status: 404,
     41	      description:
     42	        'VIDEO_NOT_FOUND — the video does not exist or belongs to another channel',
     43	      schema: { $ref: getSchemaPath(ApiErrorEnvelope) },
     44	    }),
     45	    ApiResponse({
     46	      status: 409,
     47	      description: notReadyDescription,
     48	      schema: { $ref: getSchemaPath(ApiErrorEnvelope) },
     49	    }),
     50	  );
     51	}

**Updated:**
- `openapi.json`, regenerated
- `package.json`: the `openapi:export` script
- `src/videos/decorators/api-delivery-redirect.decorator.ts`
- `src/videos/videos.controller.ts`
- `src/videos/dto/video-response.dto.ts`
- `src/openapi-export.integration-spec.ts`: 21 new tests
- `test/swagger.e2e-spec.ts`: 1 new test

**What the contract and tests now cover:**
- `VideoResponseDto` declares `status` as `draft | processing | ready | failed`, and the processing fields as nullable.
- The three redirect routes are documented as `302` with `Location` and `Cache-Control`.
- With `SWAGGER_ENABLED=true`, `/api/docs-json` lists the video endpoints.
- A test fails if the committed `openapi.json` is missing any of the 8 paths.

**Decision for you:** I changed how the file is exported. The `openapi:export` script ran through `ts-node`, where the Swagger plugin that reads the validation decorators never runs. As a result:
- **Empty request bodies:** every request body was exported with no fields. That's already true in the committed file for `RegisterDto`, `LoginDto` and the other auth DTOs, and would have been true for the new `CreateVideoDto` and `CreatePartUrlsDto`.
- **Mismatch:** the live `/api/docs` was complete, so the file and the running app didn't match, which the OpenAPI decision says must not happen.
- **The change:** the script is now `nest build && node dist/openapi-export.js`. The command you run is the same.
- **Drawback:** it rebuilds `dist/`, so it shouldn't run while `start:dev` is writing there.
- **Rejected alternative:** adding `@ApiProperty` to request DTOs, which goes against the project's DTO rule.

**Changes to existing entries in `openapi.json` (all corrections):**
- The 6 auth request bodies gain their fields.
- `GET /auth/confirm-email` now documents its required `token` parameter.
- `GET /` now declares its string response.

**Two smaller fixes:**
- **Stray `200` on the redirect routes:** the Swagger plugin added it because it can't see a response decorator inside my shared decorator. Each handler now carries `@ApiFoundResponse` directly.
- **Inline `status` enum:** the four values used to sit in a separate `VideoStatus` schema. They're now listed inside `VideoResponseDto`, as the acceptance criterion asks.

- **Download:** the response has `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="large.mp4"`.
- **Thumbnail:** the redirect returns a JPEG no wider than 1280 px.
- **Resume:** after part 1, the test throws away the client's state, asks `GET /videos/:id/upload` what's missing (`[2]`), uploads only part 2, and the video becomes `ready`.
- **Rejected codec:** an MPEG-4 Part 2 file ends `failed` with `unsupported_format`, and its stream returns `409 VIDEO_NOT_READY`.
- **Slugs:** the 3 videos created in the file have distinct slugs.

**Notes:**
- **Large test video:** generated as a lossless 720p H.264/AAC clip whose length doubles until it's over 68 MiB, so the upload always has exactly 2 parts.
- **Queue cleanup:** the worker also registers the 15-minute cleanup schedule on startup, so the test clears both queues before and after.
- **Download check:** reads just 1 byte (`Range: bytes=0-0`) to check the header without downloading about 70 MB.

     39	
     40	## Object Storage — Local Filesystem
     41	
     42	**Strategy:** Local filesystem storage in development and tests. S3 in production.
     43	
     44	**Approach:**
     45	- The storage layer should use an abstraction (e.g., `StorageService` interface) that allows switching between local filesystem and S3
     46	- In tests, use the local filesystem adapter — no mocking needed
     47	- Use a temporary directory for test uploads: `os.tmpdir()` or a dedicated `test-uploads/` directory
     48	- Clean up test files in `afterAll`
     49	
     50	**Setup pattern:**
     51	```typescript
     52	// In test module setup
     53	{
     54	  provide: 'STORAGE_CONFIG',
     55	  useValue: {
     56	    driver: 'local',
     57	    basePath: path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'streamtube-test-uploads'),
     58	  },
     59	}
     60	```
     61	
     62	**Integration test:**
     63	```typescript
     64	import * as fs from 'fs';
     65	import * as path from 'path';
     66	import * as os from 'os';
     67	
     68	describe('StorageService (integration)', () => {
     69	  const testDir = path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'streamtube-test-uploads');
     70	
     71	  afterAll(() => {
     72	    fs.rmSync(testDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
     73	  });
     74	
     75	  it('should upload and retrieve a file', async () => {
     76	    const buffer = Buffer.from('test content');
     77	    const key = await storageService.upload(buffer, 'test.txt');
     78	
     79	    const retrieved = await storageService.get(key);
     80	    expect(retrieved.toString()).toBe('test content');
     81	  });
     82	});
     83	```

     94	- For publisher tests: assert the job is  … (+995 chars)

**Flakiness:** the SI-03.3 timeout in `auth.service.integration-spec.ts` did not come back, neither in the two full runs nor in three extra runs of that file alone. Since you said to fix it only if it reappeared, I didn't change the code. The risk is still there, though.
- **Evidence:** that file's runtime swung from 6.2 s to 17.6 s between identical runs, and its setup time from 2.4 s to 11.2 s. The Docker VM is shared with other projects' containers, and the tests do CPU-heavy password hashing.
- **Mechanism:** each of its 9 `beforeAll` hooks builds a full module and syncs the database schema within Jest's default 5-second limit, and its `afterAll` crashes if that setup timed out.
- **Proposed fix (small):** give that file an explicit hook timeout, as two other spec files already do; close the test module in `afterAll`; and set up the schema once per file instead of once per `describe`.

**Follow-ups noted during the phase** (exact text is in `progress.md`):
1. Triage the 51 vulnerabilities `npm audit` reported (SI-03.1).
2. Remove the `ioredis` override in `package.json` once TypeORM accepts ioredis 6 (SI-03.1).
3. Investigate Node's `--localstorage-file` warning, which appears on every Jest run (SI-03.5).
4. The test cleanup helper assumes the `videos` table exists, so it would fail on a completely empty database (SI-03.6).
5. The shared duplicate-key check matches the column name anywhere in the error text, not just the column field (SI-03.7).
6. Older integration specs still register `User` manually when they compile `VideosModule` or `VideoProcessingModule` on their own; the worker itself is fixed (SI-03.8 to SI-03.16).
7. Tests share the dev database, so running them wipes dev data (SI-03.17).
8. The retry-5-times-then-`processing_error` path is covered by unit tests only; it wasn't exercised against a real storage outage (SI-03.16).
9. Some code samples in the testing guide fall back to `localhost`, which contradicts the Docker networking rule (SI-03.20).
